aubergine moment -->\n  <section class=\"spotlight\" id=\"spotlight\" data-invert=\"\" data-screen-label=\"04 Artist spotlight\">\n    <div class=\"spotlight-grid\">\n      <figure class=\"spotlight-portrait\">\n        <div class=\"ph tone-aubergine ph-reveal has-img\" data-label=\"studio portrait — north light\"><img src=\"assets\u002Fshelf\u002Ffront\u002Fstudio-portrait.png\" alt=\"\" style=\"position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover\">\n          <div class=\"ph-inner\" data-speed=\"0.94\"><\u002Fdiv>\n        <\u002Fdiv>\n      <\u002Ffigure>\n      <div>\n        <span class=\"micro fade-up\">Artist spotlight · 06<\u002Fspan>\n        <blockquote class=\"spotlight-quote\" data-reveal-lines=\"\">\n          <span class=\"rv-line\">“I paint the room<\u002Fspan>\n          <span class=\"rv-line\">before anyone<\u002Fspan>\n          <span class=\"rv-line\">walks in.”<\u002Fspan>\n        <\u002Fblockquote>\n        <p class=\"spotlight-bio fade-up\">Born 1991 in Cluj. Studied at UNARTE Bucharest. Currently working from a corner studio in the old Lipscani district, where the light comes through one north-facing window at four o’clock. Her practice circles around thresholds — dusk, dawn, the empty room.<\u002Fp>\n        <span class=\"spotlight-name fade-up\">andra kovács<\u002Fspan>\n        <br>\n        <a class=\"spotlight-cta href=\"#\u002F\">É-mishto<\u002Fa><span>\u002F<\u002Fspan><span>FAQ<\u002Fspan><\u002Fdiv>\n  <h1 class=\"page-title\" style=\"font-size:clamp(44px,6vw,104px);margin-top:22px;\">Questions,<br><em>answered.<\u002Fem><\u002Fh1>\n\n  <div class=\"faq-group fade-up\">\n    <span class=\"micro\">For collectors<\u002Fspan>\n    <details class=\"faq-q\"><summary>Is every object one of one?<span class=\"sign\">+<\u002Fspan><\u002Fsummary><div class=\"faq-a\">Most are. The vast majority of what you find here is unique or made in very small, numbered editions. Each listing tells you exactly how many exist.<\u002Fdiv><\u002Fdetails>\n    <details class=\"faq-q\"><summary>How do I know a creator is real?<span class=\"sign\">+<\u002Fspan><\u002Fsummary><div class=\"faq-a\">Every creator is reviewed by our curators before they can sell. We read their story, see their work, and only open the door when it feels right. Nothing is automated.<\u002Fdiv><\u002Fdetails>\n    <details class=\"faq-q\"><summary>Can I commission something?<span class=\"sign\">+<\u002Fspan><\u002Fsummary><div class=\"faq-a\">Often, yes. If a creator takes commissions it’s noted on their page — reach out through the object and we’ll introduce you.<\u002Fdiv><\u002Fdetails>\n  <\u002Fdiv>\n\n  <div class=\"faq-group fade-up\">\n    <span class=\"micro\">Shipping &amp; care<\u002Fspan>\n    <details class=\"faq-q\"><summary>How is my object shipped?<span class=\"sign\">+<\u002Fspan><\u002Fsummary><div class=\"faq-a\">Fragile and one-of-a-kind pieces are crated and insured, door to door, through our logistics partners. Smaller objects ship in handmade packaging. Every order carries the creator’s handwritten note.<\u002Fdiv><\u002Fdetails>\n    <details class=\"faq-q\"><summary>How long will it take to arrive?<span class=\"sign\">+<\u002Fspan><\u002Fsummary><div class=\"faq-a\">It depends where you are — typically two to four days domestically, five to eight across the EU, and eight to fourteen internationally. Express, white-glove delivery is offered at checkout.<\u002Fdiv><\u002Fdetails>\n    <details class=\"faq-q\"><summary>What if something arrives damaged?<span class=\"sign\">+<\u002Fspan><\u002Fsummary><div class=\"faq-a\">Tell us within fourteen days and we’ll make it right — repair, replace where possible, or refund. Insured delivery means you are never left carrying the loss.<\u002Fdiv><\u002Fdetails>\n  <\u002Fdiv>\n\n  <div class=\"faq-group fade-up\">\n    <span class=\"micro\">For creators<\u002Fspan>\n    <details class=\"faq-q\"><summary>How do I sell on É-mishto?<span class=\"sign\">+<\u002Fspan><\u002Fsummary><div class=\"faq-a\">Apply through the maker door. The application is a short, thoughtful conversation — about two minutes — followed by our editorial review. Passing the quiz means you’re eligible; the real selection is human.<\u002Fdiv><\u002Fdetails>\n    <details class=\"faq-q\"><summary>What does it cost?<span class=\"sign\">+<\u002Fspan><\u002Fsummary><div class=\"faq-a\">There’s no fee to apply or to list.
